# Graph Report - Vibe-Trading  (2026-08-23)

## Corpus Check
- 2223 files · ~2,485,182 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 35588 nodes · 76799 edges · 1272 communities (956 shown, 316 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1729 edges (avg confidence: 0.92)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `67e562a2`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
